# Phase 1 coverage ledger: projects and placements

Generated by `node scripts/audit/phase1-ledger.mjs`. Evidence is structural: a function is "referenced" when its name appears in a test file; a route is "referenced" when a test mentions its route path. This is a floor, not proof of behavioural coverage (happy path, permission matrix, tenant isolation, state machines, idempotency, audit events and error mapping must still be read in the referenced tests).
